NOT browser-verified. **Not committed / not published** — reaching online games needs a runtime release (`runtime:lines`); the editor half deploys with the launcher; standalone `bookofborut` must bump its engine submodule (the `packages/*` half) — its per-game `src/components/WinVisual.svelte` needed no edit here. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/boundComponentCatalog.ts` (new `hostedComponentSpace`), `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/LayoutNodeView.svelte` (runtime game-frame override), `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorCanvas.svelte` (editor `nodeTransform` + `isGameSpaceNode` override).
- 2026-07-27 — **the DESKTOP bake (`bake-editor-doc.mjs`) now embeds flow-v2, so a standalone build of a flow-v2 game drives from the flow like the online runtime does.** Symptom (owner, `bookofborutremakebuild`): the launcher-built standalone rendered the scenes as _placed in the Scene Editor_ — no flow driving, reel not mounted — "as if the flow was never built", while the online `bookofborutremake` (runtime:lines, live fetch) ran the flow correctly. Root cause: two different bake paths had drifted. The ONLINE runtime bundle (`runtimeBundle.ts`) assembles `flow` **and** `flowV2` + `flowV2Library` (via `exportEditorFlowV2`), but the desktop bake `bake-editor-doc.mjs` only ever called `/api/editor/export-flow` (v1) — **zero** flow-v2 handling. The game reads `bakedBundle.flowV2` / `flowV2Library` (`apps/lines/src/editor-scenes.ts` `bakedFlowV2Doc()`/`bakedFlowV2Library()`); the remake authors a v2 flow, so the desktop baked bundle embedded nothing v2 → the v2 interpreter stayed inert → static placement. **Fix, two files:** (a) the `export-flow` endpoint now also calls `exportEditorFlowV2` and returns `flowV2` + `flowV2Library` alongside the v1 `flow` (one bake call covers both; each export self-gates on "authored", so a v1/coded game gets `{}` → byte-identical parity); (b) `bake-editor-doc.mjs` captures those from the response and embeds them in the baked bundle behind the same `...(flowV2 ? …)` parity spread as every other authored block. Reaches a standalone game only after its engine submodule is bumped to this commit + rebuilt; the endpoint half deploys with the launcher. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` green; bake script `node --check` clean. ⏳ End-to-end verify: rebuild `bookofborutremakebuild` from the launcher (deploy token present) → its baked bundle carries `flowV2` → the standalone drives from the flow.
- 2026-07-24 — **`scripts/new-game.mjs` produced a game that could not build — FIVE bugs, all fixed and verified end-to-end.** Found while standing up a real standalone build for the `test1` project; each one was only reachable after fixing the one before it, so a single smoke-build of the scaffold would have caught the lot years earlier. **(1) Broken tsconfig.** The scaffold wrote `"extends": "./engine/packages/config-ts/svelte.json"`. That file does not exist — `config-ts` ships `base.json` — and `vite:esbuild` fails the build outright on an unresolvable `extends`, so it died on the FIRST module (`src/routes/+layout.ts`) with `failed to resolve "extends"`. Every game the script has ever scaffolded was unbuildable out of the box. Now emits `"extends": "config-ts/base.json"` with the `include: ["."] / exclude: [dist,build,node_modules]` shape every shipped game (`apps/lines`, Book of Borut, HotFruits) already uses — resolved by PACKAGE NAME, since `config-ts` is a `workspace:*` dep. **(2) Dependency drift → invalid compiled JS.** Svelte declares its parser/printer as CARET ranges (`esrap: ^2.0.0`, `acorn: ^8.12.1`, `@sveltejs/acorn-typescript: ^1.0.5`) and a brand-new standalone repo has **no lockfile**, so a fresh install floats them to latest while the monorepo stays on what its committed lockfile resolved (`esrap` 2.0.1 / `acorn` 8.15.0 / `acorn-typescript` 1.0.5). The drift is not cosmetic: **esrap 2.3.0 prints the TypeScript optional-parameter marker into the compiled JS** — `components-ui-pixi/UI.svelte`'s `{#snippet gameName(override?: HudTextOverride)}` came out as `function gameName($payload, override?)`, which is not valid JavaScript, and rollup died with `Expected ',', got '?'`. Identical Svelte version (5.35.1) and byte-identical `svelte/compiler` in both trees — the bug was one layer below it, which is why every config-level comparison (svelte.config/vite.config/pnpm-workspace/tsconfig/plugin list/`ssr.noExternal`) came back identical. The scaffold now emits a `pnpm.overrides` block pinning those three; **re-check them when bumping svelte**. Also added `engine-flow`, `engine-flow-v2`, `engine-fx` to the scaffold's dependency list — `apps/lines` needs all three, so "copy `src/` from an existing game" (what the script's own closing message tells you to do) previously required hand-editing `package.json`. **(3) `svelte` was stale at 5.20.5** while every engine package declares 5.35.1 — and svelte + esrap + acorn are ONE set, so the pins in (2) paired with 5.20.5 blew up on SvelteKit's OWN `error.svelte` with `Not implemented: Program` (an esrap failure). Bumped to 5.35.1 to match the engine. **When bumping svelte, bump the overrides with it.** **(4) No `lingui.config.ts`.** `config-vite` unconditionally registers the `lingui()` plugin, which aborts with `No Lingui config found` when the file is absent; the scaffold never wrote one. Now emits the same one-liner (`import config from 'config-lingui'`) every shipped game has. **(5) No `src/routes/+layout.ts`.** `config-svelte` uses `adapter-static`, which needs `prerender = true`; without it the build got all the way through both vite passes and then died at the adapter with `Encountered dynamic routes`. Now emits `prerender = true` / `ssr = false` (the game is a client-side pixi canvas) / `trailingSlash = 'ignore'`, matching the standalone games. **Verified end-to-end:** scaffold → `pnpm install` → `pnpm build` exits 0 and writes `build/index.html` (59 KB) — previously it failed at the first module. Note for anyone copying `apps/lines/src` into a standalone repo: take it from the **committed `main`** the submodule pins, not the working tree — a dirty engine tree exports symbols (e.g. `resolveSymbolName`) that the pinned engine does not.

Shipped together with the `/symbols` save fix that made the setting persistable at all (see `docs/status/symbols.md`) — until that landed, NO `winCycle` field ever reached R2.

- 2026-07-24 — **The free-spin intro/outro VISUALS get the same WYSIWYG fix as the win overlay** (same bug class, found while fixing the win overlay below; applied pre-emptively before the owner authors them). `FreeSpinAnimation.svelte` — the shared frame block behind BOTH `FreeSpinIntroVisual` and `FreeSpinOutroVisual` — hardcoded `width={PANEL_SIZES.width}` PLUS an authoring-box offset (`x: 0.5·PANEL.width`, `y: 0.4·PANEL.height`) that applied even under `boundToInstance`, so an authored instance could never match the editor preview (which draws the rig at natural size at the node's transform). Fix mirrors `WinAnimation` exactly: new optional `width?`; `frameWidth = width ?? (boundToInstance ? undefined : PANEL_SIZES.width)` so the coded/OFF composer keeps the historical fit and the AUTHORED path renders NATURAL size; `loadScaleBase={EDITOR_SPINE_LOAD_SCALE}` only on the natural-size path (the engine-bundled `fsIntro` loads at `parser.scale` 2 vs the editor's 1, and Spine leaves `data.width/height` unscaled, so nothing else cancels it); and the authored branch drops the offset + centring pivot entirely so the rig's SKELETON ORIGIN sits on the instance node's origin. **The offset/pivot pair compensated for nothing:** offset (300, 240) vs pivot (300, 234.78) ⇒ net (0, +5.2px), and no sibling reads it — the count spine + caption sprites render INSIDE the frame's `slot_text_placeholder` and ride the RIG, not the container (their `sizes.width * 0.3/0.4` are frame-RELATIVE and were deliberately left alone). **Parity:** OFF path is byte-identical (`frameWidth` resolves to the former `PANEL_SIZES.width`, offsets unchanged, `loadScaleBase` undefined ⇒ factor 1, the `<MainContainer>` wrapper untouched); `apps/{cluster,scatter,ways,price}` have no `FreeSpinAnimation`. The two Visual components needed NO edit — `PANEL_SIZES` is `FreeSpinAnimation`'s own private constant and it already receives `boundToInstance`. Verified: `pixi-svelte` + `engine-layout` + `lines` builds green, `svelte-check apps/lines` 0 errors, Prettier clean. NOT browser-verified. **Known remaining FS divergence (NOT fixed):** the frame rig is hardcoded `key="fsIntro"` for BOTH intro and outro, while the defs' spine params (`introSpine`/`outroSpine`, default `fsIntroNumber`/`fsOutroNumber`) name the COUNT rig — and `instancePreviewSpineBundle` hands the editor the FIRST spine param, so the editor can preview the number rig where the runtime draws the frame rig. Making it unconditional needs a `frameSpine` param listed first on both defs (separate change).
- 2026-07-24 — **An authored `win` Overlay instance now renders at the size + place the Scene Editor previews (WYSIWYG).** Owner report: the `win` componentInstance was resized in the editor so the rig fit nicely; in-game the big-win rig came out WAY too small and offset. Root cause was **two different BASE sizes for the same rig**, with the node's scale multiplying whichever one it landed on. RUNTIME: `apps/lines/src/components/WinAnimation.svelte` hard-coded `<SpineProvider width={boardLayout().width}>`, so the rig was always squeezed to the board width and the instance scale shrank it further. EDITOR: `EditorSpineLayer.svelte`'s `bindSpineTarget` passes the bind node's own `width`/`height` — which the `WIN_DEF` `win-anim` node does not set — so the preview draws the rig at its NATURAL size and the instance transform sizes it. A rig authored ~2500px wide previewed at 2500 and shipped at ~600 ⇒ scaling it down to taste in the editor made the in-game copy tiny. Fix: `WinAnimation` takes an explicit `width?`; `WinVisual.svelte` passes `boardLayout().width` ONLY on the coded/OFF composer path (`bind:Win`, `boundToInstance={false}`) and passes nothing on the authored instance path, so both surfaces size from the same natural base and the node transform is the single knob. **POSITION needed no separate fix — it was the same bug.** Verified the two compose identically: the `win-visual` instance is a `componentInstance` with no `bind`, so the editor's `nodeTransform` never applies the catalog `placement:'boardCentre'` (that only fires for a `bind`/`preview.art` anchor — `resolveAnchorPreviewArt` returns undefined without `node.bind`), and its nested `win-anim` bind composes purely through `composeWorldMatrix`/`childLocalTransform`; the runtime walks the identical chain (`<MainContainer>` → instance `<Container>` → `win-root` → `win-anim` `<Container>` → `WinVisual` at LOCAL origin) and `<SpineProvider>` with no `anchor` pivots at 0, so both put the SKELETON ORIGIN at the same point. What moved was the art: `bigwin`'s skeleton canvas is `x:-200 y:-200 1600×1600` — **not** origin-centred — so scaling about the origin at two different base sizes displaces the visual centre. One base ⇒ one position. **Load-scale reconciliation (the second half, and the trap):** the Spine readers scale skeleton GEOMETRY but copy `skeleton.data.width/height` through UN-scaled (confirmed in `SkeletonJson.js`/`SkeletonBinary.js`), so a bundle's load scale is a bare multiplier that NO sizing path cancels — a width-fit renders at `parser.scale × width`, natural size at `parser.scale × data.width`. Actual numbers: the editor previews EVERY rig at `EDITOR_SPINE_LOAD_SCALE = 1`; an exported `deploy/editor-art` spine is written with `scale: EDITOR_SPINE_LOAD_SCALE` (`editorArtExport.ts`) and read back at that same 1 by `editor-scenes.ts` ⇒ **already at parity (1 ↔ 1)** for every R2 rig, which is the online case. The engine-BUNDLED `bigwin` in `apps/lines/src/game/assets.ts` is **`scale: 2`** ⇒ 2× the editor, so "natural size" would still have meant two things for any game pointing `winSpine` at a bundled rig. Compensated explicitly rather than by changing that (which would halve the OFF path): new `packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/spineLoadScale.ts` records each bundle's `parser.scale` as `assetLoad.ts` reads it, and `<SpineProvider>` gained an opt-in `loadScaleBase` that divides the difference out (`loadScaleBase / recorded`, resolved under the same key the fallback bundle lookup lands on). `WinAnimation` passes `EDITOR_SPINE_LOAD_SCALE` on the natural-size path only. The constants moved to `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/spineLoadScale.ts` (the package BOTH surfaces already depend on) with `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/spineScale.ts` reduced to a re-export, so the editor preview and the runtime can't drift apart. **Parity:** `loadScaleBase` absent ⇒ factor 1 ⇒ every other `<SpineProvider>` in every game is byte-identical; the OFF composer keeps `boardLayout().width` and therefore its exact shipped size; `apps/{cluster,scatter,ways,price}` have no `WinVisual` at all. **Not fixed here:** `FreeSpinAnimation.svelte` has the same class of mismatch under `boundToInstance` (a hard-coded `width={PANEL_SIZES.width}` plus x/y offsets) — same fix shape, left for a separate change. **Verified:** `pnpm --filter pixi-svelte build` + `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` + `pnpm --filter lines build` + `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` green; `svelte-check apps/lines` **0 errors / 0 warnings** (pinned `typescript@5.8.3` + `svelte-check@4.3.0`); Prettier clean. NOT verified in a browser. **Not committed / not published** — reaching online games needs a runtime release (`runtime:lines`), and the launcher needs a deploy for the re-exported constants; standalone `bookofborut` must mirror the per-game `src/components/{WinAnimation,WinVisual}.svelte` (the `packages/*` half rides an engine submodule bump).
- 2026-07-24 (rebased onto `main` 2026-09-18 — the bug was still there) — **A game that is not Book-of can trigger free spins at last: the shared mock RGS had no feature at all.** Owner report: 3 scatters landed in the online game `test1` and nothing happened, "it works fine in book of borut remake". Entirely server-side. `publishGame` maps the project's game kind to a mock (`protocolFor`: `bookOf` → `book`, everything else → the shared `scripts/mock-rgs-server.mjs`), and that mock implemented scatters as a **pay only** (`SCATTER_PAY_TABLE`, 3 SCAT → 2× stake — exactly the $1 bet / $2 win in the report). It emitted no `spinTrigger`/`enterBonus`, so the round closed on the trigger spin. Book of Borut works because it is registered `protocol: 'book'` and the book-of mock does trigger. The fix does NOT make `test1` a Book-of game (owner: "test1 needs to be Line, not book Of"): the shared mock gained **its own scatter-triggered free-spin feature** — 3+ SCAT awards `TOTAL_FS`=10 spins, the round STAYS OPEN, each `play` returns one free spin with `bonusWin`/`spinWin`/`playedSpin`/`playedBonusSpin`, 3+ SCAT during a free spin retriggers `RETRIGGER_FS`=5 (chaining without limit), and `gameEnd` is emitted only when `left` hits 0 — which is what makes the facade's drive loop terminate instead of hanging. **No book-of mechanic:** the mock never emits `pickRandomly`, so no `setExpandingSymbol` and no `expandBookColumns`; verified optional — `stakeFacade` gates the expansion on `specialRaw` and `bookEventHandlerMap.setExpandingSymbol` is an independent handler. No client change was needed: `engineFacade` drives the whole bonus off `enterBonus` (`while (!gameEnd) post play` then `collect`) and `apps/lines` already carries every free-spin component. **The mock now serves four win models, so this reaches them all** — lines, ways, cluster and scatter alike, since the SCAT pass runs for every model. It is **self-gating on the project's own symbol set**: `scatterEnabled` is false when a restricted pool omits SCAT, so a game with no scatter never deals one and never opens a feature. **The trigger spin does not tumble** — the cascade is a base-game presentation fixture, and a chain there would rewrite the win the free-spin counter then accumulates; `cascade-chain.fixture.mjs` was taught the new shape (a trigger round holds the round open, enters the bonus, emits no `tumbleStep`). `FORCE_TRIGGER=1` / `opts.forceTrigger` forces the feature for testing, and forces it on the BASE spin only, so free spins still retrigger by chance. **Money agrees with #405, which landed first and rewrote how this mock prices a win.** A free spin scores through `evaluatePayWins` → `payoutBaseFor`, so it uses the same per-model base as a base spin (per line / per way / whole bet) and is rounded to whole cents by the same `roundPays`; the SCAT pay is priced against the whole stake and rounded identically in both paths; and `pendingRound.win` accumulates the ALREADY-ROUNDED pays, so the round total is the exact sum of the wins the client was shown. Calling an evaluator directly instead — which is what this branch originally did — prices every free spin per LINE, which on a paylines-less model (`ways`/`cluster`/`scatter`, which declare none since #405) pays **nothing but the SCAT**. ⚠️ **`check:stake` cannot see that**: `finalWin` and `payoutMultiplier` both derive from `gameEnd.win`, so they agree with each other however the wins were priced — the mistake leaves every assertion green and merely pays less. It is pinned instead by `check:freespins` §5, confirmed to go red on the injected bug (0 ways wins vs 42). **Verified:** `scripts/check-freespin-protocol.mjs` (new, `pnpm check:freespins`, now inside `pnpm check:rgs` so CI runs it, 28 assertions over the real HTTP handler driven exactly as `engineFacade` drives it) — forced trigger gives 10 counters ending `played=10 left=0`, exactly ONE `gameEnd`, a collect that pays the accumulated win, every free-spin win mirrored by a `bonusWin`; a retrigger round runs 10+5×N spins on one `gameEnd`; 80 unforced rounds give 0 malformed; a SCAT-less pool deals 0 scatters and opens 0 features. `check:stake` gained a fifth model (`ways + free spins`, forced) — **20 paying FEATURE rounds, 0 desynced, 122 wins, 0 fractional, 0 zero-pay**, which is the aggregated-round settlement the base-game models could not cover. Full `pnpm check:rgs` = 129 assertions green. **Parity against the post-#405 `main`:** with SCAT out of play (the feature impossible) 240 rounds across all four models are **byte-identical** — pays, `gameEnd.win` and balance; with SCAT in play every round before the first feature is identical too (lines 6/6, ways 60/60, cluster 11/11, scatter 12/12), and the stream then legitimately diverges because a feature round deals ten extra boards. Natural trigger rate ≈ 1 feature per 43 base spins (600-round survey). **Ships with a `services/test-server` deploy** (Railway) — server-side only, so NO runtime-bundle release is needed. ⏳ Not yet confirmed in the browser on `test1`; and if that project's v2 flow drives screens but authors no free-spin screens, the spins will run state-only with no intro visual (`freeSpinTrigger` runs `presentIntro=false`). ⚠️ Unrelated pre-existing red: `scripts/smoke-mock.mjs` fails on its first assertion ("heartbeat should return no events") on `main` too — it predates the boot `config` event and was not touched here.
- 2026-07-24 — **The HUD no longer mounts TWICE under a v2 flow that drives the screens.** Owner report: an intermittent "glitch" on the balance text — an extra half-glyph hanging off the end of `$97.00`. Not a font bug: **every HUD readout was rendered twice**. Verified live on `test1` (`games.invisiblewall.org/test1`, shared `_runtime/lines` bundle): the stage carried TWO bands at zIndex 102, each a full copy of the 15-node `hudBar` scene, with two `$100.00` `Text` objects at the identical global x=145, both `anchor {x:0.5,y:0.5}`, w=161. The doc declares ONE `hudBar` scene and the FlowDoc ONE `hudBar` container, so this was the engine mounting it from two places. **Why it read as a glyph artifact:** each copy owns its own `svelte/motion` count-up `Tween` (one via `<ParamReadoutText>`, one via the coded label path), so mid-change the two hold different strings — `$100.00` is 161px wide, `$97.00` ~131px — and centre anchoring makes the wider one overhang the narrower by half the difference on each side. It self-heals once both tweens settle, which is why it looked transient. **Root cause:** `<UI>` renders the `hudBar`/`hudCorners` scenes ITSELF (`<UI hud={{ bar, corners }}>`), and a driven v2 flow mounts those same scenes as containers through `<FlowV2Mount>`. Neither guard on the `<UI>` block caught it — `isHudFlowManaged` reads the **v1** mounter only (`flow?.mounter.authoredScreenIds()`), and `suppressCodedHud` is `hasAuthoredHud()`, deliberately keyed on `hud_*`-prefixed scenes and never the canonical `hudBar`. Its two sibling blocks already carry the right guard (`authoredHud` and `extraScenes` are both `{#if !flowV2DrivesScreens}`, each commented "mounting them here too would double them"); the coded `<UI>` gate never got it. **Fix:** `&& !flowV2DrivesScreens` on `Game.svelte`'s `<UI>` condition, so under a driven flow `<FlowV2Mount>` is the sole scene renderer as documented. **Parity:** a game with no flow, a book-events-only flow, or a half-on flow has `flowV2DrivesScreens` false ⇒ byte-identical. **Verified live** after a `runtime:lines` release (CI published `bundle.7Ja7QjAI.js`, the exact file the game serves): the stage went from 6 `$`-readouts across two z=102 bands to **3 across one**. ⚠️ **Behaviour change under a driven flow — the coded `<UI>` chrome goes with it:** the BUY BONUS button, and the `hudCorners` game-name/logo. The `hudCorners` container still mounts (the flow shows it) but renders EMPTY, because its `bind: { component: 'HudGameName' | 'HudLogo' }` nodes are rendered only by `<UI>`'s `gameName`/`logo` snippets — `boundComponentCatalog` declares their params but no `<LayoutScene>` renderer exists. So a flow-driven game currently has NO way to render those two binds. Menu/auto/turbo/± survive on `test1` because the author already authored them as `Button_Image` instances. **Open:** give `HudGameName`/`HudLogo` (and a buy-bonus bind) a `<LayoutScene>`-side renderer so a driven `hudCorners` paints, or drop them from the reference `hudCorners` scaffold. Reaches online games via a runtime release (`runtime:lines`); standalone `bookofborut`'s `Game.svelte` is a pre-flow-v2 divergent copy with no `flowV2DrivesScreens` and cannot hit this bug.
- 2026-07-24 — **The runtime now scales the DOC's canvas box, so an authored screen can no longer render at a different size/position in the game than in the Scene Editor.** Owner report: "a big difference between the way I have things in the scene editor and the way they display in game — a size difference and a placement difference … the free spin intro / book reveal is perfect". That last clause is the tell: the free-spin intro is a `canvas`-space screen (raw window pixels), and canvas space is the ONE space that never touches the main box. Root cause: two surfaces disagreed about which box a `game`-space node is authored in. The Scene Editor previews against `data.doc.mainSizesMap` (the right panel's **Canvas Size**, editable per layoutType), but the runtime scaled a box **hard-coded** in the game's `stateLayout.ts` (`apps/lines`: desktop 1422×800) — `createLayout` captured it in a closure at module scope and NOTHING anywhere read `doc.mainSizesMap`. `<MainContainer>` still filled the window either way, so a drifted box was silent: every `game`-space node came out scaled by `min(cw,ch / codedBox)` instead of `…/docBox` **and** offset, because a node's position is measured from the box centre (`mainToWorld`) — one wrong number moves and resizes everything at once. A doc can drift trivially: `editorStorage.ts`'s `DEFAULT_MAIN_SIZES` (desktop 1920×1080, landscape 1024×576, …) is nobody's game box, and the Canvas Size field is free-form. Fix: `utils-layout/createLayout.svelte.ts` gained a module-level `$state` authored box + `setAuthoredMainSizesMap()` / `getAuthoredMainSizesMap()`, consulted **only** by `mainLayout` (the game box) and never by `mainLayoutStandard` (the fixed 1920×1080 HUD box every game shares). `apps/lines/src/editor-scenes.ts` publishes it from `loadEditorScenes()`, which is the ONE funnel all four doc sources return through (live runtime bundle / baked freeze / `/api/editor/doc` / bundled fallback), so no source can be missed. Validation is per-layoutType: a non-finite/≤0/missing entry falls back to the coded box rather than collapsing the stage. **Parity:** every reference layout (`lines`, `bookof`, `engineSkeleton`) and the seed script already declare exactly 1422×800 / 1000×1000 / 1600×900 / 800×1422 — identical to the coded map — so a game whose doc box already matches is byte-identical. **Behaviour change for a DRIFTED doc:** the game now renders the box the author previewed, which also re-scales the coded parts laid out relative to it (`boardLayout()` derives from `mainLayout().width/height`, so the reel board stays centred but resizes with the box) — that is the point: the editor becomes the truth. The editor's mismatch warning was reworded to match (it no longer claims "the game will render at a different size"; it now warns that the coded reel board follows YOUR box, not the reference's) — note it lives inside the **collapsed** "Canvas Size" panel, which is why a drifted project can run for a long time unnoticed. **Verified** in a running `apps/lines` (WebGPU, 1280×720): at boot `getAuthoredMainSizesMap()` returns the doc's map and `<MainContainer>` sits at pivot 711/400, scale 0.9 = min(1280/1422, 720/800) — unchanged; calling `setAuthoredMainSizesMap({desktop:960×540,…})` live re-derived it to pivot 480/270, scale 1.3333 = min(1280/960, 720/540), proving the box is doc-driven and reactive. `pnpm --filter lines build` green. Reaches online games via a runtime release (`runtime:lines`); standalone `bookofborut` needs the same two-line wiring in its OWN `editor-scenes.ts` (its `src/game/*` is not delivered by the submodule).
- 2026-07-24 — **Turbo is no longer overridden by the free-spin sequential-stop mode.** Owner report: turbo works in the base game ("the board is spinning faster as it should") and stops working the moment free spins start — "getting overwritten by the new playmode of the free spin". Root cause in `packages/utils-slots/src/createEnhanceBoardSpin.ts`: the global spin type is `stateBet.isTurbo ? 'fast' : 'normal'`, but `forceSequentialStop` (set for the whole feature by the Flow `enableSequentialReelStop` effect) was applied **after** it — `useSequential = forceSequentialStop && !isAnticipated` forced every non-anticipated reel to the `sequential` spinType and `noStop = true`. That discarded turbo twice over: `sequential` is not a `fast` spinType, so `reelState.spinOptions()` fell back to `SPIN_OPTIONS_DEFAULT` (the slow profile) instead of `SPIN_OPTIONS_FAST`, and `noStop: true` also vetoed `generalSpinWith`'s `(!noStop && isTurbo && isSpinning)` instant-land branch in `createReelForSpinning`. Fix: `&& !stateBet.isTurbo` on `useSequential`, so a held turbo returns the reel to the global `fast` type. Anticipation still wins over both (unchanged). No-op for a game that never authors `enableSequentialReelStop` (the mode is off ⇒ `useSequential` was already false) and no-op with turbo off ⇒ byte-parity for the dev games. ⏳ Live-verify with turbo held through a free-spin feature on a game whose FlowDoc authors the effect. Reaches online games via a runtime release (`runtime:lines`); standalone `bookofborut` picks it up on an engine submodule bump.
- 2026-07-24 — **A bitmap font that under-reports its own glyph box no longer ignores `fontSize` (and no longer overlaps its lines).** Owner report on the `/symbols` win-line text: "the font size … is not working, at least not with the default silver font. I put it at 0.25 but is still super big, and is also overlapping the 2 lines". Not a win-line bug — a FONT-DESCRIPTOR bug the call site cannot reach. pixi 8 derives everything about a static bitmap font from two descriptor numbers (verified against `pixi.js@8.8.1` source): `getBitmapTextLayout` draws at `scale = style.fontSize / font.baseMeasurementFontSize` (`<info size>`) and advances each line by `font.lineHeight` (`<common lineHeight>`); `style.lineHeight` is never consulted for bitmap text. The engine's bundled **`silver`** declares `size=97 lineHeight=97` for glyphs that are actually **199** tall — confirmed on the LIVE font object in a running `apps/lines` (`silver: lineHeight=97 base=97 glyphBox=199`, while `gold` 105/105/105, `purple` 177/177/177, `goldblur` 376/376/376 all agree). So at the authored `size: 0.25` (⇒ `fontSize = SYMBOL_SIZE * 0.25 = 30`) silver drew its glyphs **54px** tall (1.8× the requested size — "still super big") while advancing only **30px** per line ⇒ the message and the amount **overlap**. New `normalizeBitmapFontMetrics(font)` in `packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/sanitizeBitmapText.ts` (sibling of the existing black-screen guards, exported from the package) measures the font's real glyph box (`max(yOffset + texture.frame.height)`) and, when it overflows the declared line box by >10%, rescales `lineHeight`, `baseMeasurementFontSize` and `baseLineOffset` by that factor — restoring the invariant every correct descriptor already satisfies (`lineHeight ≈ tallest glyph`), so text draws at the requested `fontSize` and one line clears the next. Called from `pixi-svelte`'s `<BitmapText>` alongside `ensureBitmapFontSpaceGlyph`/`sanitizeBitmapText` (before the PIXI object is constructed, so the FIRST frame is already correct), and from the launcher's `EditorTextLayer.svelte` bitmap path so the editor canvas and the game agree on what `fontSize` means. Idempotent (per-font `WeakSet`) and a **no-op for a correct descriptor** — gold/goldblur/purple are byte-identical — and for a DYNAMIC font (glyphs minted from a real font file; metrics correct by construction, char set still growing). ⚠️ **Behaviour change for existing silver text:** any authored size tuned against the broken metrics now renders ~1.8× smaller (it finally honours the number) — re-tune those. **Verified:** a Node fixture replicating pixi's exact `getBitmapTextLayout`/`BitmapTextPipe` math over the four real `.xml` descriptors (silver 54.1px glyph / 30px pitch / OVERLAP=true → 26.4px / 30px / OVERLAP=false; the other three unchanged, corrected=false), plus the live browser check above where calling the shipped function on the loaded `silver` font moved it 97/97 → 199/199. `pnpm --filter pixi-svelte build` + `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` green; `tsc --noEmit --strict` clean on the new module. Reaches online games via a runtime release (`runtime:lines`); standalone `bookofborut` picks it up on an engine submodule bump.
- 2026-07-24 (later still, ROOT CAUSE) — **the sequential replay showed only the last line on the SHIPPED games, because the number of `winInfo` events per spin is a property of the SOURCE BOOK.** `recordWinCycleWins` ASSIGNED `wins = bookEvent.wins`, which is right for the reference books (`apps/lines` fixtures put every win in ONE event, `wins: [w1,w2,w3]`) and wrong for the Play4Fun facade the shipped Book of Borut actually runs on: `engineFacade.adaptEventsForEngine`'s `flushWins()` pushes one `winInfo` PER win (`reveal → winInfo×N → setTotalWin`), so each event overwrote the previous and only the LAST line survived. This is why local `apps/lines` testing looked correct while the remake did not — a reminder that a fixture book and the live facade book are different shapes. Fix: `recordWinCycleWins` now ACCUMULATES within a spin (`reveal` still clears), de-duplicated by a `winKey` (symbol|kind|lineIndex|positions) so a book emitting per-line events AND a summary event can't list a line twice. Both book shapes now produce the same rotation. **Verified against LIVE data**, not just the code: eight real `POST /rgs/engine` bets against the book-of mock each returned **3 `spinWin` events** (⇒ 3 `winInfo`s), and in-browser the three-separate-events shape rotates all three groups (`0:1,1:2,2:3` → `0:3,1:2,2:3` → `0:2,1:2,2:2`) with a repeated flush adding no fourth entry. `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors; build green.
- 2026-07-24 (later still) — **the resting replay STEPS THROUGH the paying lines** (owner: "if I have multiple lines … go through all the winning lines sequentially; right now is looping only the last win"). The symbols-only cut lit the deduped UNION of every winning cell each pass, so a three-line spin read as one undifferentiated block. `winSymbolCycle` now keeps one `Position[]` group PER win (`cycleGroups()`, book order, empty groups dropped) and animates them one at a time with the authored gap between each, looping back to the first after the last — a single-win spin is the degenerate one-entry rotation. Note the wins all arrive in ONE `winInfo` event (`wins[]`, each with `meta.lineIndex`), so nothing changed about recording; only the playback shape did. `/symbols` slider relabelled "Gap between lines". **Verified in the browser** (dev + mock RGS, three overlapping lines): the cue log rotates `0:1,1:2,2:3` → `0:3,1:2,2:3` → `0:2,1:2,2:2` → back to the first, ~435ms apart, ZERO `winLineShow`/`winLineHide`, nothing after `stopWinCycle()`. `tsc --noEmit` over `apps/lines` 0 errors; both builds green.
- 2026-07-24 (later, OWNER CORRECTION) — **the resting replay is SYMBOLS ONLY.** The first cut (next entry) replayed the whole `winInfo` presentation, so the line and its stamped amount redrew on every pass — "this is wrong and was never asked, only the symbols animation should have looped". Reworked: `winLineCycle.ts` → **`winSymbolCycle.ts`**, which broadcasts NO `winLineShow`/`winLineHide` at all (whatever the round left on screen stays as it was) and awaits only `animateSymbols`. It also stopped stepping win-by-win: with no line to say WHICH win is on show, sequential groups just read as symbols blinking, so one pass lights the deduped UNION of every winning cell in the spin. The `winLineEnabledForWin` filter went with it — that gate is about the LINE (it excludes scatter wins and reads the line's on/off toggle), and a scatter win's symbols animate during the round like any other. Config moved OUT of the win-line style block to a doc-level **`winCycle: { enabled?, delay? }`** with its own `bakedWinCycleConfig()` + its own "Winning symbols after the spin" section in `/symbols`, because a replay that never draws the line must not be governed by the line's toggle (`winLine.loop`/`loopDelay` are gone; nothing had authored them yet). **Verified in the browser** (dev + mock RGS, two overlapping wins): the cue log shows only repeating `boardWithAnimateSymbols` over the deduped union `0:1,1:1,2:1,1:2,2:2` every ~450ms, ZERO `winLineShow`/`winLineHide`, and nothing after `stopWinCycle()`. `tsc --noEmit` over `apps/lines` 0 errors; both builds green.
- 2026-07-24 — **Win lines now CYCLE on the resting board until the next spin** (owner request: "they only play once and then go back to static"). `winInfo` presents each win exactly once, so the board sat static from the end of the round until the next bet. New `apps/lines/src/game/winLineCycle.ts` replays the LAST spin's wins on a loop: per pass it broadcasts `winLineShow` (points + amount/message from the shared `winLinePointsFor`/`winLineTextFor`), awaits `animateSymbols`, broadcasts `winLineHide`, waits `loopDelay` (default 0.4s, floored at 120ms so a subscriber-less cue can't busy-loop), and repeats — so a cycled pass is byte-identical to the round's own pass, including the scatter exclusion (`winLineEnabledForWin`) and the `/symbols` on/off toggle. Driven from `playBet` (`apps/lines/src/game/utils.ts`): `stopWinLineCycle()` FIRST, so a new bet never rolls the reels under a stale line, and `void startWinLineCycle()` in the `finally` after the slam token resets (so even a slammed round gets its full line back at rest). Wins are recorded in `dispatchBookEvent` — ahead of the coded/v1/v2 branch, since a flow-owned `winInfo` never reaches the handler map — with `reveal` clearing, so a free-spin feature cycles its LAST spin. A `generation` token makes start/stop idempotent and stops a stale pass wiping a live line. Skipped while `stateBetDerived.isContinuousBet()` (autoplay >1 / space hold), where the next spin is already on its way. Authored in `/symbols` → Win lines → Line as **Loop until next spin** + **Loop gap**, sparse (`winLine.loop` only persists when OFF), resolved by `bakedWinLineConfig()` (`loop` default true, `loopDelay` 0.4) and passed through the exporter verbatim. **Verified in the browser** (`apps/lines` dev + mock RGS): the cycle emitted show→animate→hide→~420ms gap repeatedly for as long as it was left running, and `stopWinLineCycle()` ended it with a single `winLineHide` and silence after. `tsc --noEmit` over `apps/lines` = 0 errors; `pnpm --filter lines build` + `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` green. Reaches online games via a runtime release (`runtime:lines`); standalone `bookofborut` needs the `src/game/{winLineCycle,utils}.ts` mirror.

Book of Borut only worked because its `freeSpinOutro` scene binds the `FreeSpinOutro` COMPOSER (gate+visual) inside a shown container. **Fix (`apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`):** mount `FreeSpinOutroGate` ENGINE-OWNED under a driven flow — the count-up/state/round-block DRIVER — mirroring the WIN engine-mount. Placed at `freeSpinOutroZIndex` (`sceneLayerZIndex('freeSpinOutro')`, the outro container's OWN band) and BEFORE `<FlowV2Mount>`, so its dim scrim sits BEHIND the authored `FreeSpinOutroVisual` (same-z, insertion-order tiebreak) exactly as the WIN gate sits below the win visual — no dim burying the spine, no double dim. **Exactly one `freeSpinOutroCountUp` `waitForResolve` subscriber in every path** (two hang the round): a new `freeSpinOutroHasCodedGate` derived (`fsOutroScene.nodes.some(bind.component === 'FreeSpinOutro' | 'FreeSpinOutroGate')`, analog of `basegameOverlaysHasCodedWinGate`) skips the engine gate whenever the shown scene STILL binds the composer or a bare gate (Book of Borut ⇒ TRUE ⇒ engine gate stands down, live outro untouched); the driven seed binds only the visual ⇒ FALSE ⇒ engine gate is the sole driver. **Divergence from the WIN gate (deliberate, documented):** `FreeSpinOutroGate` does NOT self-suppress its press/dim under flow the way `WinGate` does — it has no `OnMount` self-resolve (its round-block releases ONLY on the tap, per `flowEffects`), and it is the SAME component Borut's composer mounts, so forcing `codedPressOwned`-style suppression would both HANG this round (nothing would release the hold) AND strip Borut's live outro of its scrim + tap. So it mounts INTACT: its `PressToContinue` is the SOLE tap surface the driven outro has (the seed authors no `tapToContinue`), matching Borut's proven tap-to-continue UX. **Parity:** the engine gate is gated on `flowV2DrivesScreens`, so a non-flow / book-events-only game NEVER mounts it — the coded gate at `LAYER_BAND_TOP` owns it there ⇒ byte-identical to today. **Verified:** `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` + `PUBLIC_RGS_TRANSPORT=play4fun pnpm --filter lines build` green; `svelte-check apps/lines` 0 errors (`typescript@5.8.3` + `svelte-check@4.3.0`). NOT verified in a browser (no baked driven-seed game to spin headlessly). **Reaches online Borut via a runtime release (`runtime:lines`).** Standalone desktop `bookofborut`'s `src/components/Game.svelte` is a PRE-flow-v2 divergent copy (no `flowV2DrivesScreens`, still carries the removed `Scene.gate` props, never got the WIN fix either) — the fix is NOT mechanically applicable there and was left untouched; that build's outro cannot hit this bug (it has no driven-screens path).

The coin fountain used to live in `WinGate.svelte` (the engine-owned DRIVER), so it hard-centred on the board via its own `MainContainer` + `boardLayout` and was neither toggleable nor movable. Now it renders as part of `WinVisual.svelte` (the positionable componentInstance VISUAL): it sits inside the SAME positioned container as the tier spine / count number, so dragging the `win` component moves the coins and its instance scale scales them. (2) **`showCoins` param** — new `{ key: 'showCoins', kind: 'boolean', default: true }` on `WIN_DEF` (`packages/engine-layout/src/lib/builtinComponents.ts`), read by `WinVisual` via `getComponentParams()['showCoins']` (new `booleanParam` helper) with a `showCoinsProp = true` prop fallback (same pattern as `winSpine`/`slotName`); `false` ⇒ no fountain. Default true ⇒ parity, and an old baked def lacking the key falls back to the prop default. (3) **`WinCoins` de-centred** — `WinCoins.svelte` dropped its own `MainContainer` + `boardLayout` wrapper (and the now-unused `getContext`); it renders just the `<ParticleEmitter>` from its LOCAL origin, so it TRULY honours the enclosing transform (no hard-centring left). A game that doesn't place the `win` component is unaffected (its `WinVisual` never mounts). Exactly one `WinCoins` in every path (moved, not duplicated). **Verified:** `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` + `pnpm --filter lines build` green; `svelte-check apps/lines` **0 errors / 0 warnings** (pinned `typescript@5.8.3` + `svelte-check@4.3.0`). A missing/mis-named animation is NON-fatal — `SpineTrack` try/catches `setAnimation` and logs the spine's available names (`SpineTrack.svelte`), and the round still completes because the coded `WinGate` owns the count-up/round-await independent of the spine. `mergeBuiltinCodedParams` unions the new params onto the already-placed pinned-v1 instance at resolve time ⇒ no re-place, no version bump. Verified: `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` + `svelte-check apps/lines` 0 errors. Standalone `bookofborut` must mirror the per-game `src/components`/`src/game` files (the `packages/engine-layout` half rides an engine submodule bump).
- 2026-07-22 — **Boot loading screen (kills the online "long black screen").** The `?runtime=1` online build showed a bare black screen for the whole pre-mount window — `+layout.ts`'s blocking `load()` awaits the cross-origin `/api/editor/runtime` fetch (Railway, cold-startable) BEFORE the page component + Pixi canvas + the in-canvas `LoadingBar` mount, so nothing could paint, then the ~2.5s R2 editor-art/symbol/font asset wave followed. Fix is an HTML-shell splash in `apps/lines/src/app.html` (inline HTML/CSS + the inlined IW emblem, no JS deps) that paints on the first frame and is driven by a tiny global `window.__ieBoot` (`title`/`phase`/`progress`/`done`): animated IW emblem + game-name + uppercase phase text (`Fetching from R2…` → `Loading assets…`) + progress bar + %. `editor-scenes.ts`'s `prepareRuntimeBundle` calls `__ieBoot.phase('Fetching from R2…')` around the fetch and sets the title from a new `name` field the runtime endpoint now returns (`projectName`); `Game.svelte` feeds `stateApp.loadingProgress` → `__ieBoot.progress` and calls `done()` (fade + remove) on `stateApp.loaded`. Additive + optional-chained ⇒ inert offline/dev/Storybook (splash flashes + dismisses instantly; no `?runtime=1` ⇒ no R2 phase). A 30s safety timeout guarantees the splash can never pin over a live game. Verified live against the prod launcher: `Fetching from R2…` + pulsing emblem + indeterminate bar show during the exact fetch window, runtime fetch succeeded (no stale fallback), overlay auto-removed on load. **Needs a runtime publish** (`runtime:lines`) to reach online games; the launcher half (`/api/editor/runtime` `name`) ships via `main` auto-deploy. Standalone `bookofborut` must mirror `app.html` + the `Game.svelte`/`editor-scenes.ts` wiring (per-game files, not `packages/*`).

Reuses the same bone-hosting mechanism `EffectNode.hostSpineId` uses for effects. Six additive fields on `SpineNode` (`packages/engine-layout/src/lib/types.ts`): `revealSymbolBone` (unset ⇒ nothing mounts, byte-parity), `revealSymbolState` (default `'bookIdle'`), `revealSymbolScale` (1), `revealSymbolOffsetX/Y` (0), `revealSymbolFollowRotation`/`revealSymbolFollowScale` (true). `LayoutNodeView` mounts the game's registered `revealSymbolRider` bound component inside the rig's `<SpineProvider>` via `<SpineBoneAttach>` when `revealSymbolBone` is set (`packages/engine-layout/src/lib/LayoutNodeView.svelte`, beside the `attachedEffects` bone-hosting block). New tiny game component `apps/lines/src/components/RevealSymbolRider.svelte` (modeled on `ExpandingSymbol` — reads `stateGame.specialSymbol`, renders it via the shared `<Symbol>` state machine; position/scale/rotation come from the host bone), registered under key `revealSymbolRider` in `apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`. **No normalize/whitelist change needed:** the launcher's `normalizeNode` (`editorStorage.ts`) trusts kind+id and passes ALL node fields through untouched — only Scene-level fields are whitelisted (the `gotcha_scene_field_whitelist_normalizescene` gotcha is Scene-scoped), so the six new node fields survive save/load with no edit. The /editor authoring UI to set the six fields is a separate task (owner-owned).
- 2026-07-20 — **The book-reveal rig (`FreeSpinIntroSymbolReveal.svelte`) played the first free-spin feature and froze on the second — a stray-`complete` RACE, distinct from the a3d16cb signal-cue bug.** Owner report: "the symbol Book reveal animation is not playing when I get a second free spin! the original rig is not resetting, I can see the delay I placed in that flow is working, but not the animation." This component drives `<SpineTrack animationName>` DIRECTLY (no replay token — `apps/lines/src/components/FreeSpinIntroSymbolReveal.svelte:134`), so a3d16cb doesn't cover it. Root cause: the reveal plays a one-shot `intro` then swaps `animationName` to a LOOPING `idle`, and Spine fires `complete` at the end of EVERY idle loop. The `complete` listener ran the intro→idle hand-off on any completion while `phase === 'intro'` (old `FreeSpinIntroSymbolReveal.svelte:139`). On a retrigger `play()` flips `phase` to 'intro' synchronously, but the `<SpineTrack>` `$effect` applies the intro a tick later; a stray idle-loop completion landing in that window ran the hand-off against an intro that never played — settling the reveal EARLY (round still unblocks — matches "the delay works") and pinning `animationName` back to `idle`, after which the value guard (`shouldApplySpineAnimation`) sees `idle === idle` and never re-applies. The rig sits frozen on idle. This is the PERSIST scenario: the live flow-v2 doc doesn't deliver `specialBookHide` to the mounted component (the coded `freeSpinEnd` that broadcasts it, `bookEventHandlerMap.ts:255`, is suppressed under flow ownership; the reference choreo authors it at `bookOfChoreo.ts:319` but the owner's doc need not), so `phase` never returns to 'hidden', `show` stays true, and **FadeContainer never fades the SpineProvider out** — `FadeContainer.svelte:30` unmounts children only at `alpha.current === 0`, and no `persistent` prop is passed, but the fade is never triggered. Failure mode (b): the animation never restarts on a rig that stays mounted; not (c). **Fix (minimal, 1 line of logic):** gate the `complete` handler on the completing animation's name — `phase === 'intro' && entry?.animation?.name === introAnimation` — so a stray idle-loop completion can't abort a fresh intro; each reveal is self-contained per `play()`. Deliberately did NOT also reset `animationName` in `hide()` (the coordinator's alternative): with the rig still mounted during a dismiss fade it would re-apply and REPLAY the intro while fading out — a visible regression — and it's unnecessary since `play()` always sets `animationName = introAnimation` and the unmount scenario resets on remount anyway. **Fixture-proven:** `packages/pixi-svelte/fixtures/introRevealRetrigger.fixture.ts` (`node --experimental-strip-types …`) models the real apply/complete lifecycle across two features, reusing the shipping `shouldApplySpineAnimation` value guard verbatim, and injects the stray idle complete in the retrigger race window. Pre-fix: `setAnimation` calls `['intro','idle']` (intro played once, ever) and feature 2 settled EARLY (`introAppliesAtSettle [true,false]`); post-fix: `['intro','idle','intro','idle']` (intro once per feature), rig ends on idle, one settle per feature, none early. Verified: `apps/lines` build green, `svelte-check` 0 errors / 2 warnings (the two pre-existing `tsconfig.json` option warnings), the a3d16cb fixture still green (no regression), and the guard confirmed REACHABLE in the built bundle (`m(L)==="intro"&&((we=me?.animation)?.name)===m(_)`). NOT verified in a browser. **Needs a runtime publish** to reach online games. **Book of Borut mirror required:** this is in a per-game component copy, so standalone `bookofborut`'s forked `src/components/FreeSpinIntroSymbolReveal.svelte` must get the same `complete`-guard edit (an engine submodule bump alone will NOT deliver it).
- 2026-07-20 — **An authored spine cue ("Plays on signal") played once per SESSION, not once per fire.** Owner report: "when I play the free spin for a second time in a row, my spine animation is not playing anymore! it works fine the first time". Root cause is engine-generic, not Book-of specific: a cue is an EVENT but is delivered to the spine as STATE. `<ComponentInstance>` wrote `signalAnims[nodeId] = { animation, loop }` on each fire (`packages/engine-layout/src/lib/ComponentInstance.svelte:398`), `<LayoutNodeView>` forwarded the name to `<SpineTrack>`, and `<SpineTrack>`'s apply guard is a VALUE comparison — `resolvedAnimationName !== track?.animation?.name` (`packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/components/SpineTrack.svelte:46`). A second fire of the same cue writes the same animation name, so the guard read "already playing" and skipped the re-apply; the finished non-looping track stayed parked on its last frame for the rest of the session. Failure mode (b): the animation never restarts, not (a) or (c) — the component stays mounted and `signalAnims` is never cleared. Fixed by adding a monotonic per-instance FIRE token: `ComponentSignalAnim.fire` (`componentSignalContext.ts`), bumped by a shared `fireCue` helper on both the game-signal subscription and the self-fired `enter` cue, forwarded by `LayoutNodeView` as `<SpineTrack replay>` only while the cue is the ACTIVE override (a button `stateAnimations` spine is declarative and keeps the plain comparison), and consumed by an extracted pure decision `shouldApplySpineAnimation` (`packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/spineTrackReplay.ts`). `replay: undefined` short-circuits to the original comparison ⇒ every uncued spine, and both coded book components (`SpecialBook`, `FreeSpinIntroSymbolReveal`, which already re-arm via their own phase machines), are byte-identical. **A fixture corrected the diagnosis mid-flight**: `packages/pixi-svelte/fixtures/spineTrackReplay.fixture.ts` (`node --experimental-strip-types …`) drives two free-spin features through the real decision function and showed a SECOND, worse pre-existing bug I had assumed away — when the cued node ALSO has a resting `defaultAnimation`, the one-shot→idle hand-off makes `props.then === track.animationName`, collapsing the guard so the cue never played AT ALL, not even the first time. The same token fixes it. Pre-fix: `['intro']` / `['idle']`; post-fix: `['intro','intro']` / `['idle','reveal','reveal']`; an uncued spine stays `['idle']`. This also pins the owner's node shape: it works once, so it is a **cue-only spine with no resting `defaultAnimation`** — most likely cued on `freeSpinStart` or `specialBookReveal`. Verified: `pixi-svelte` + `engine-layout` + `apps/lines` builds green, `svelte-check apps/lines` 0 errors / 2 warnings (both pre-existing `tsconfig.json` option warnings), and the guard confirmed REACHABLE in the built bundle (`apps/lines/build/_app/immutable/bundle.*.js` contains the minified `appliedReplay` comparison). NOT verified in a browser. **Needs a runtime publish** to reach online games; the change is entirely in `packages/*`, so standalone `bookofborut` needs only an engine submodule bump (no `src/game/*` or `src/components/*` mirror).
- 2026-07-20 — **The per-spin slam re-arm moved from `reveal` to `updateFreeSpin` (free-spin counter fix).** Owner report: "it seems like the free spin counter is not working anymore if I slam stop the reel during freespins". An ORDERING bug in the per-spin re-arm (`407e0c2`), not a render bug. A free spin's book events are `updateFreeSpin` → `reveal` → …, so the counter update LEADS the spin it labels, but `rearmSlamForSpin()` was called at the top of the `reveal` leaf. After a slam on spin N the token therefore stayed tripped across spin N+1's `updateFreeSpin`: its state write still landed (synchronous — which is why a state-level test passes and the free-spin COUNT was never wrong), but its PRESENTATION was processed under a tripped token — the counter's cues collapsed to 0ms and, under a screen-driving flow, a `showContainer{awaitComplete}` on that chain was auto-completed by the slam-aware mount (350b473) so the following `hideContainer` took the counter down. A player who slams every spin — the normal way slam is used — loses the counter presentation for the whole remainder of the feature. Fixed by keying the re-arm to the book event that STARTS a free spin: new `SPIN_REARM_BOOK_EVENTS` (`updateFreeSpin`) applied by `runBookEventPresentation` BEFORE the dispatch (`apps/lines/src/game/unskippablePresentation.ts`), so it covers the coded, v1-flow and v2-flow paths at one seam and is fresh ahead of the spin's first cue; the calls in the coded `reveal` handler and the `revealBoard` flow effect are removed (their `isBonusGame` guard STAYS — it still governs `stopButtonEnable` and `recordBookEvent`, which genuinely belong to the reveal). **Scoping verified against the data, not assumed**: across all 10,100 reference books `updateFreeSpin` never appears without a `freeSpinTrigger`, never before one, and exactly once per free spin (13 reveals = 1 base + 12 free spins, 12 updates) — so the base game never re-arms mid-round and keeps its round-scoped slam, and a RETRIGGER needs no special case because extra spins arrive as more `updateFreeSpin` events. The resume replay (`createBonusSnapshot`) re-arms once on an already-reset token ⇒ no-op. **No hang**: the change only ever un-trips the token, which can restore a wait but never strand one. Verified: `apps/lines` build green, `svelte-check` 0 errors / 2 warnings (identical to the stashed-main baseline), and a new PRESENTATION-level harness `tools/flow-spike/slamCounter.ts` (`pnpm --filter flow-spike run slamcounter`) driving `BOOK_OF_CHOREO` through `runFlowEvent` against the real `roundSkip`/`awaitCue`/`runBookEventPresentation` plus a copy of the slam-aware mount — 8/8 assertions, measuring token state at dispatch and observed cue DURATION rather than state. It reproduces the bug against pre-fix `main` (spin #3 collapsed at 0ms and its hold auto-completed; with `SLAM_EVERY=1`, spins #3–#11) and passes with the fix at every slam timing. NOT verified in a browser. **Needs a runtime publish** to reach online games + a mirror into standalone `bookofborut`.
  - Shipped alongside it: **`showWinInfoMessage` is now fault-isolated** (`apps/lines/src/game/flowEffects.ts`). This is a SEPARATE defect on its own merit, NOT the counter root cause. It runs inside the awaited book-event chain and touches data the game doesn't control end-to-end (`Intl` via the currency formatter, the baked win-text doc, an authored template); a throw propagated out of `sequence` and aborted `playBookEvents`, silently dropping every remaining book event — the counter frozen on the spin the round died on, `freeSpinEnd` never run, the game left in `freegame` with no outro. A presentation-only toast must never cost the player the rest of the round, so it now degrades to "showed nothing" and logs. Found while bisecting the counter report; no production trigger identified (a bad currency would break the unslammed `winLineTextFor` path too).
- 2026-07-20 — **A slam now shows a MINIMUM WIN DISPLAY instead of a blank fast-forward.** Owner report: "the slam stop seems to work correctly for the free spin, but I still would like the game to show the win symbols after the stop (if there is a win) … but also I would like to have the win info message to appear for each win". Collapsing every wait to zero ran the whole `winInfo` sequence inside a couple of frames: the symbols lit and the toast fired, but were overwritten before a frame painted, so a winning slammed spin read as "balance moved, nothing happened". Three edits, all in `apps/lines/src/game`: (a) **`unskippablePresentation.ts`** gains `SLAM_MINIMUM_DISPLAY_CUES` (`boardWithAnimateSymbols`), `SLAM_SYMBOL_HOLD_MS` = 200, `SLAM_MESSAGE_HOLD_MS` = 400 and `slamHold()`; `awaitCue` now holds for the symbol beat when the token is ALREADY tripped and the cue owns a minimum display, instead of collapsing. That cue is the single seam all three dispatch paths funnel their win-symbol animation through (coded `animateSymbols`, v1 flow `broadcastAsync`, v2 flow cue `broadcast`), so the lit symbols come back everywhere WITHOUT touching `Board.svelte` — its subscriber still runs detached and still lands on `postWinStatic` by itself, so nothing can stay lit forever. (b) **`flowEffects.ts`** — `winLineEnabledForWin` is false while slammed (the line and its stamped amount ARE the beat the press asked to skip), `hideWinLine` made unconditional so a press landing between a win's show and its hide can't strand a drawn line, and the `showMessage` text resolution lifted into a new exported `showWinInfoMessage()` shared with the coded handler; the effect holds `SLAM_MESSAGE_HOLD_MS` after a message when slammed, so a multi-win spin's messages read one after another rather than overwriting each other in one frame. (c) **`bookEventHandlerMap.ts`** — the coded `winInfo` handler shows that same per-win message on a slammed spin only (the coded path never had one; both reference choreographies author it as a `showMessage` node, and a flow that owns `winInfo` still owns it). **Pacing: 600ms per win** (200 symbols + 400 message) — a 3-win spin is ~1.8s against ~4.8s unslammed, and the message is readable. **Unslammed is byte-unchanged**: every new branch is behind `roundSkip.isSkipped()`, and the one unconditional change (`hideWinLine`) replaces a no-op with a no-op. **No hang**: both holds are bare `waitForTimeout` timers (deliberately NOT `roundSkip.wait`, which would collapse), so they settle on their own schedule with no dependency on a subscriber, a spine `complete` or a player press — i.e. on nothing the slam suppressed. **Money is untouched**: `setTotalWin`'s value write and `WinCountUpProvider`'s `onSkip` jump-to-final are unchanged. NOT verified in a browser. **Needs a runtime publish** to reach online games + a mirror into standalone `bookofborut` (it forks `src/game/*`).

A single-reveal base-game book never calls it ⇒ **base game unchanged**. Every consumer already reads the token at start-of-work (`createReelForSpinning`'s roll, `createEnhanceBoardSpin`'s anticipation arm, `WinCountUpProvider`'s duration), so a re-arm restores full pacing for free. **`spinStop.ts` needed no logic change**: `isSkipped()` now means "this spin is already slammed", so two presses within one spin still cancel autoplay — a cancel is always one extra press away, in whichever spin is playing (comment updated to say so). - 2026-07-20 — **Slam stop: the book reveal and the free-spin intro are now UNSKIPPABLE.** Owner report: "the free spin intro and book reveal can't be stopped! … they skip (but keep on playing) and the game starts while the irig for the book reveal still playing". Racing a wait against `roundSkip` ends the WAIT, not the ANIMATION: for a count-up or a win line that is fine (the skip lands the picture at its final value), but the book/expanding-symbol reveal and the free-spin intro are spine/`.irig` TIMELINES that nobody cancels, so the round chain walked on while the rig kept playing over the next free spin's reels. New `apps/lines/src/game/unskippablePresentation.ts`: `UNSKIPPABLE_BOOK_EVENTS` (`setExpandingSymbol`, `freeSpinTrigger`) + a depth-scoped `runBookEventPresentation`, opened in `playBookEvent` around the WHOLE dispatch so it covers the coded, v1-flow and v2-flow paths alike; `awaitCue` (every awaited `broadcastAsync` in the game now goes through it — `bookEventHandlerMap`, `flowEffects.awaitPresentation`, `flowRuntime`'s `broadcastAsync`, `flowV2Runtime`'s cue `broadcast`) and `waitPresentation` (both runtimes' `waitForTimeout`) drop the race inside that scope. Scoped by BOOK EVENT, not cue name, because `transition`/`uiHide`/`uiShow` punctuate the free-spin END too and that must keep fast-forwarding. Ordering falls out for free: book events are strictly serial and each handler fully awaited (`createPlayBookUtils`), so the first free-spin `reveal` cannot start its board work until the reveal cue's subscribers have settled. **THE HANG RULE** — `PLAYER_GATED_CUES` (`freeSpinIntroUpdate`, `bookRevealGateShow`, `freeSpinOutroCountUp`) keep racing even inside the scope, and flow-v2's `showContainer{awaitComplete}` keeps its `roundSkip.onSkip → complete` release (350b473 NOT regressed): each is held by a `PressToContinue`, and after a slam the spin button is inert and drawn above the overlay, so it swallows the very tap that would release it. The free-spin intro is therefore unskippable in everything except its tap-to-continue hold. Base-game slam, the reel snap and the rest of the win presentation are untouched. Verified: `apps/lines` build green, `svelte-check` 0 errors, and a Node fixture bundling the REAL `skipToken.ts` + `unskippablePresentation.ts` (16/16) proving each unskippable cue settles with the token already tripped, that it does not release before its subscribers, the reveal→next-event ordering, that every player-gated cue still releases, and that outside the scope the fast-forward is unchanged. NOT verified in a browser.
- 2026-07-20 — **Win presentation layers as HUD, not board furniture.** The win line and the amount it stamps rendered inside the board `<MainContainer>` at the implicit z 0, so every list-ordered screen in `LAYER_BAND_BASE` (the HUD, `basegameOverlays`, an author's above-reel art) painted over them. `<WinLine>` now mounts as its own board-space layer at the new fixed `LAYER_BAND_WIN_PRESENTATION` (8000, `engine-layout/layerOrder.ts`) — above that whole band, still below the pinned `LAYER_BAND_TAKEOVER` celebrations and the engine's `LAYER_BAND_TOP` round-blocking gates, so nothing an author layers can bury the line but a gate still covers it. It keeps its own `<MainContainer>` wrapper (it positions through `<BoardContainer>` in board-local coords and needs the game-space transform its old parent supplied); `<MainContainer>` is pure derived layout, so the second instance reproduces the identical transform and the line lands in the same PLACE — only higher in the stack. Still inside the base-game gate, so it unmounts with the board as before. Shipped to `_runtime/lines` (run 29734003384; verified live on `bookofborutremake` / `bundle.BWzPyPRC.js`) and **confirmed good in a live spin by the owner**. Standalone Book of Borut is NOT covered: it keeps its own `Game.svelte`, which still mounts `<WinLine>` inside the board `<MainContainer>` — the submodule delivers the constant but the markup edit must be mirrored there by hand.
- 2026-07-20 — **Slam stop: the free-spin outro hold now releases on a slam (hang fix).** Owner report: "the game breaks during free spin if I slam stop just before the free spin win". `showContainer{awaitComplete}` — the flow-v2 round-block hold, shipped as a KNOWN gap with af71840 — was the ONLY await left in the round chain that a slam could not release: every cue, delay and cut-short effect races `roundSkip`, but that hold is resolved solely by a tap on a `tapToContinue` overlay. In a flow-driven game the free-spin outro IS that tap gate, so a slam fast-forwarded the whole feature and then parked on it. Two things made it a dead end rather than a wait: `runSpinOrSlamStop` early-returns once the token is tripped, so the spin button the player presses next does nothing, and that button is drawn above the overlay, so it swallows the very tap that would have released the hold. The round then never completes ⇒ `playBet` never returns ⇒ the machine never reaches idle. `playBet`'s `try/finally` cannot save it: an un-settled await is not an exception, so the `finally` never runs. Fixed in `apps/lines/src/game/flowV2Runtime.svelte.ts` with a slam-aware mount wrapper — `awaitComplete` registers the hold, then subscribes `roundSkip.onSkip` to `mount.complete(id)` (subscribed AFTER registration, because `onSkip` fires synchronously on an already-tripped token). Completing is exactly what the tap does, so the same exec chain resumes show → hide → next and no beat, state write or win is dropped. The wrapper is applied unconditionally; the `?flowlog=1` trace wrapper now delegates through it (it previously wrapped `rawMount`, so a fix placed there would only have existed with tracing on). **Coded and v1-flow paths were never affected** — every hold in `bookEventHandlerMap`/`flowEffects`/`flowRuntime` is raced, and a Node harness driving the real svelte `Tween` + `interruptible` + `skipToken` on a fake raf clock confirmed `reel.spin()` settles for a slam at every point in the roll AND for a reel whose spin STARTS with the token already tripped (the next free-spin reveal), so the fully-awaited `enhancedBoard.spin()` cannot strand the round. Only apps/lines runs flow-v2 ⇒ no other app affected. **Needs a runtime publish to reach online games** (shared `_runtime/lines` bundle) + a submodule bump for standalone `bookofborut`.
- 2026-07-20 — **Slam stop implemented end-to-end (reels + win presentation).** It was ~60% built but gated to autoplay: `ButtonBetProvider.getKey()` returned `spin_disabled` for a rolling SINGLE bet ("a single bet's roll has nothing to stop"), and the identical logic was duplicated verbatim in `Game.svelte`. Four faults, all fixed:
  - **The button was inert.** The key/press decision now lives ONCE in `packages/utils-shared/spinStop.ts` (`getSpinButtonKey` / `runSpinOrSlamStop`), shared by `ButtonBetProvider.svelte`, `Game.svelte`'s registered `spin` action and its replacement Space hotkey. A rolling round shows a live STOP. The `stopDisabled` latch (which made a second press a no-op) is GONE — a second press has to be able to cancel autoplay. `SpinButtonKey` lost its `stop_disabled` member, which nothing could produce.
  - **Anticipating reels were un-slammable.** `createReelForSpinning`'s `noStop` branch awaited `slideDown()` OUTSIDE `interruptible.add`, so an anticipation reel could not be interrupted at all. It now always routes through the interrupt, with an explicit slam (`roundSkip.isSkipped()`) overriding `noStop` — which also covers the reel that has not STARTED rolling when the press lands, something `interruptible` cannot do (it only cuts an in-flight wait). `reel.stop()` clears `anticipating`, and `createEnhanceBoardSpin` no longer ARMS a new anticipation on a slammed round.
  - **The post-reveal phase was not skippable.** New primitive `packages/utils-shared/skipToken.ts` — `createSkipToken()` + the `roundSkip` singleton: `isSkipped/skip/reset/onSkip/wait/race`. STICKY (`onSkip` fires immediately for a late subscriber), which is what lets ONE press fast-forward everything that mounts after it. `runSpinOrSlamStop` trips it SYNCHRONOUSLY before broadcasting `stopButtonClick`, so no subscriber-ordering dependency exists. Re-armed per ROUND in `playBet` and nowhere else — a bonus book is one round, so one press covers the whole feature. `WinCountUpProvider` finishes on skip (and starts at duration 0 if already slammed) so count-ups land on the FINAL total; `WinLine` draws complete; the hard-coded waits and awaited broadcasts in `bookEventHandlerMap`/`flowEffects` are raced/`roundSkip.wait`-ed. Book events are still ALL run and each handler still fully awaited — racing a handler would let a detached `reveal` keep spinning the reels under the `winInfo` that follows.
  - **Flow-driven presentations ignored it.** Both flow hosts inject `waitForTimeout: roundSkip.wait` and race their awaited broadcasts. The flow EFFECT registry is invoked directly by the interpreter and bypasses that wrapper, so every awaited broadcast in `flowEffects.ts` now goes through a local `awaitPresentation` helper. The registry itself is deliberately NOT raced: on a whole-feature skip that would resolve `revealBoard` instantly for all ten spins and run ten reveals concurrently on the same reels. `FlowEmitter.broadcastAsync` was narrowed `Promise<unknown[]>` → `Promise<unknown>` (the results were never read) so the game can wrap it.
  - No XState change was needed: with the waits collapsed, `playGame` resolves fast and the machine drains to idle on its own.
  - **Turbo side effect checked (`ButtonTurbo`):** `stopButtonClick` still flips a NON-persistent turbo on and `stopButtonEnable` flips it off; `updateIsTurbo(_, {persistent:false})` is a no-op while `isTurboLocked`, so a player-set turbo is never corrupted. `playBet` now broadcasts `stopButtonEnable` from a `finally`, so a throwing handler can no longer leave turbo stuck on.
  - **One press, one action.** The spin button's Space hotkey now stands down while a press-to-continue overlay is mounted (`stateUi.continuePressCount` / `hasContinuePress()`, armed by each game's `PressToContinue`) — otherwise one keypress ran both the slam and the continue-press, with `soundPressBet` over the outro music. The pointer needs no such gate: the overlay's full-screen rect and the button are separate hit targets, so a click reaches exactly one, and both fast-forward the presentation.
  - **Blast radius (shared code).** `spinStop`/`ButtonBetProvider`, the `createEnhanceBoardSpin` anticipation guard, `WinCountUpProvider`'s skip hook and `PressToContinue`'s press claim are shared, so ALL six dev games get the live single-spin STOP and the two-press autoplay cancel. `createReelForSpinning` (the `noStop`-inside-`interruptible` change + `stop()` clearing `anticipating`) reaches only **lines** and **price** — `cluster`/`ways`/`scatter`/`number-picker` use `createReelForCascading`, which is untouched. Because `roundSkip` is a process-wide singleton, EVERY app's `playBet` had to be given the per-round `reset()`; without it one slam would have stayed tripped for the rest of the session (no anticipation would ever arm again, every count-up instant). The presentation fast-forward itself is still `apps/lines` only.
  - **Known gaps:** ~~a flow-v2 `showContainer{awaitComplete}` round-block hold (tap-to-continue) is NOT released by a slam~~ — this shipped as a HANG (the free-spin outro never released); fixed the same day, see the entry above. `UI_FEATURES_UK` is now an incomplete UK profile (UK prohibits player-led spin-stop but slam is unconditional); noted in `stateUi.svelte.ts`. The never-assigned `onCountUpComplete` dead prop was removed in `lines`' `Win`/`FreeSpinOutroGate` (and `WinCountUpProvider.oncomplete` made optional), but the same dead pattern remains in the other apps' copies.
  - **Not published** to `_runtime/lines`, and Book of Borut's engine submodule not bumped — an engine change reaches an online game only through the runtime-release chain.
- 2026-07-17 — **Book-of expanding special paid nothing unless it happened to form a left-aligned run from reel 1.** The book-of mock RGS (`scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs` — which IS Borut's live RGS) expanded the free-spin special onto its reels but then evaluated the expanded board **strictly on the paylines**, so a special on non-adjacent reels, or on any reels not starting at reel 1, formed no run and paid zero (reported from a spin with the book/cactus on three reels that returned nothing). The special is the Book-of-Ra expanding symbol: it now pays **scatter-style on the COUNT OF REELS it covers** (adjacency-independent), at `PAY_TABLE_LINE[special][reelCount] × totalStake` — which equals `mult × betPerLine × NUM_LINES`, the symbol paying that N-of-a-kind on all ten lines at once. New `evaluateExpandingSpecial`; `expandSpecialBoard` still runs so the OTHER symbols pay their normal line wins on the expanded board (the special itself excluded from that pass, never double-paid). Gate is now **reels covered, not raw symbol count**: 3+ reels, except `PIC1` (the top symbol) which expands from 2 since it pays from a 2-of-a-kind. The client morph gate in `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming/src/engineFacade.ts` was changed in lockstep (same `PIC1`=2 / else=3 reel gate) so the reels that visibly morph are exactly the reels that pay. Validated deterministically against the reported board and the gate edges (PIC1@2, PIC2@2→null, ACE@5) — the live mock's reels are random so a browser spin can't reproduce the exact board. Standalone Borut carries its own `scripts/` copy, so this must be mirrored there before it ships.
- 2026-07-17 — **Four free-spin presentation faults, all silent by construction.** Reported as "the reveal symbol never appears, the counter says `10 OF` on spin one, the book symbol vanished, the outro plays nothing".
  - **`SpineBoneAttach` could not detect a missing bone.** `follow()` probed the result of `getBonePosition(name, outPos)` for truthiness, but spine-pixi returns the passed-in scratch point UNCHANGED on a miss (it only `console.error`s). So `pos` was always truthy, the documented "never silently vanish" fallback was **dead code**, and a bad/unset bone re-applied the world transform to the previous frame's leftover `bonePoint` every tick — compounding drift plus an error per frame. Now resolves the bone first and genuinely falls back. The bone is also resolved once per frame instead of twice.
  - **The free-spin counter had no writer on the trigger chain.** `freeSpinCounterCurrent` is only ever written by `updateFreeSpinCounter` (on `updateFreeSpin`), which does not arrive until the first spin has RESOLVED — so `freeSpinCounterShow` put the counter on screen reading a value nobody wrote (0 on a first session; the previous session's final count on a repeat, since `exitFreeSpinOutro` never reset it). `setFreeSpinCounterTotal`/`…TotalOnly` now seed `current = 1` and `exitFreeSpinOutro` resets it to 0.
  - **The `freeSpins` composed string bypassed the finite guard** its own siblings use (`freeSpinsCurrent`/`freeSpinsRemaining` → `freeSpinCounterValues.ts`), reading `stateUi` raw. It was the one readout that could still render a non-number at the player, and because the `gold` BMFont carries no letter glyphs, `NaN OF 10` dropped to a BLANK rather than visibly reading "NaN" — presenting as a layout fault instead of a wiring one. Now routed through the guard.
  - **The free-spin outro animation never re-armed.** `SpineTrack`'s `complete` parks `animationName` on `idleAnimation`, and the `$effect` that sets it only refires when `outroAnimation` CHANGES — which it never does. So the authored outro animation ran exactly once per page load and every later outro opened already parked on idle. `freeSpinOutroShow` now re-arms it.
  - **Not published** to `_runtime/lines` yet at time of writing — see the runtime-release note below; an engine fix reaches an online game only through that chain.
  - **Authoring note (not an engine fault):** placing any node in the `specialBook` scene flips `hasAuthoredBookReveal` ⇒ `Game.svelte` strips the coded `SpecialBook` anchor, which is why authoring a reveal rig "removes" the book symbol. Working as designed; the reveal is expected to carry the symbol itself (via `symbolBone`).
- 2026-07-17 — **Full-screen camera effects (shake / flash / zoom punch / chromatic wobble), authorable from Flow.** New `pixi-svelte` `cameraEffects` module driving the `Application.stage` + one `cameraEffect` vocab action with a `kind` enum dropdown. Verified against the LIVE `apps/lines` stage (`?flowV2=lines`) by pumping `app.ticker.update()` by hand — the Browser pane backgrounds the tab, so rAF never fires and the Pixi ticker sits frozen at 0ms; a naive `await` on any ticker-driven effect just times out there. All four kinds hit their reference strength and restore the stage to exactly `(0,0)`/scale 1 with the ticker listener released; all four compose simultaneously and drain independently; `intensity: 999` clamps; an unknown `kind` no-ops instead of throwing. The real registry was exercised through Vite's module cache (`import('/src/game/flowEffects.ts')` → same singleton as `window.__PIXI_APP__`), confirming `blocking:true` returns a Promise that resolves only after frames run and the default returns `undefined`. **Not yet published** to `_runtime/lines`, and no FlowDoc authors it yet.
  - **Gotcha found in review — `Container.filterArea` is LOCAL, not global.** `FilterSystem` does `bounds.addRect(filterArea)` then `bounds.applyMatrix(container.worldTransform)` (`filters/FilterSystem.mjs:63-66`). Setting `stage.filterArea = app.screen` therefore makes the filter bounds RIDE any stage transform — so a `chromaticWobble` composed with a `shake` clipped an unrendered strip off the screen edge, oscillating at the shake frequency (24px offset ⇒ 24px blank strip; measured). Chroma _alone_ is fine (identity transform), which is exactly why it survived the first live pass. The rect is now recomputed per frame as the inverse-transformed viewport, verified to 0px drift under a live shake with a control proving the check fails against the old code. Anything else that filters a transformed container faces the same trap.
- 2026-07-15 — **Idle-bound rig FX no longer clipped by the reel mask.** A rig-timeline effect (`RiggedEffect`) authored to fire on a symbol's IDLE animation was clipped to the reel window, because idle symbols render on the MASKED board layer and `RiggedEffect` parents its FX subtree on the host spine (a descendant of the mask). New generic pixi-svelte seam: `FxPortalProvider` mounts one UNMASKED sibling container + provides it via a new `getContextFxPortal()`/`setContextFxPortal()` context; `RiggedEffect`, when that context is present, parents its `fxParent` into the portal and per-frame mirrors the host spine's WORLD transform onto it (`setFromMatrix(portalWorld⁻¹ · spineWorld)`) so the effect is transform-identical to the spine-parented default but no longer clipped. `apps/lines/Board.svelte` wraps ONLY the masked (`animate={false}`) layer in `FxPortalProvider`; the win/animate layer is already unmasked and left unwrapped, so the win path + all non-portalled `RiggedEffect` usages (layout spines, tools) are byte-identical. Symbol ART stays masked. **Not yet published** to `_runtime/lines` (needs the runtime-release chain).
- 2026-07-14 — Free-spin intro: a screen-driving v2 flow is the sole presentation authority; the coded intro no longer half-fires when the flow drops the intro ([detail in history](../history.md)).
- 2026-07-14 — Win-line renderer ported into the shared engine (`806d6cf`) — every `runtime:lines` game now draws the authored payline + win amount ([detail in history](../history.md)).
- 2026-07-13 — Free-spin sequential reel stop shipped to `_runtime/lines` + Borut engine bump (`6004374`) ([detail in history](../history.md)).
- 2026-07-03 — Three-knob reel grid; shared `CatalogText` gives coded HUD text authored bitmap fonts ([detail in history](../history.md)).
